- **local-llm-agent-loop-temperature-zero-trap**: Setting `temperature=0` for a tool-calling agent loop on a smaller local LLM (qwen3-coder:30b, Llama-3 30B-class, glm-4.7-flash 29.9B etc.) tightens variance BUT cuts mean accuracy hard — sometimes 5–10×. Greedy decoding locks the model into a wrong first-token path that subsequent rounds can't escape. The same sampling that creates run-to-run noise is also what gives the loop room to self-correct. ([blas1n/claude-skills](https://github.com/blas1n/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/local-llm-agent-loop-temperature-zero-trap) · ★2)
